1	import {
2	  cloneSendResult,
3	  cloneSendStateSnapshot,
4	  type ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
5	  type SendResult,
6	  type SendServiceStatus,
7	  type SendStateSnapshot,
8	  type SendStatisticsSnapshot,
9	  type SendStatsDelta,
10	} from '../core';
11	
12	const MAX_RECENT_RESULTS = 100;
13	
14	function emptyStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
15	  return {
16	    totalRequests: 0,
17	    totalSent: 0,
18	    totalErrors: 0,
19	    totalBytesSent: 0,
20	    byFrame: {},
21	    byTarget: {},
22	    byResultKind: {},
23	  };
24	}
25	
26	function emptySnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot {
27	  return {
28	    status: 'idle',
29	    statistics: emptyStatistics(),
30	    recentResults: [],
31	  };
32	}
33	
34	function applyStatsDelta(stats: SendStatisticsSnapshot, delta: SendStatsDelta): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
35	  const isSent = delta.resultKind === 'sent';
36	  const byFrame = { ...stats.byFrame };
37	  const prevFrame = byFrame[delta.frameId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };
38	  byFrame[delta.frameId] = {
39	    sent: prevFrame.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
40	    errors: prevFrame.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
41	  };
42	
43	  const byTarget = { ...stats.byTarget };
44	  const prevTarget = byTarget[delta.targetId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };
45	  byTarget[delta.targetId] = {
46	    sent: prevTarget.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
47	    errors: prevTarget.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
48	  };
49	
50	  const byResultKind = { ...stats.byResultKind };
51	  byResultKind[delta.resultKind] = (byResultKind[delta.resultKind] ?? 0) + 1;
52	
53	  return {
54	    totalRequests: stats.totalRequests + 1,
55	    totalSent: stats.totalSent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
56	    totalErrors: stats.totalErrors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
57	    totalBytesSent: stats.totalBytesSent + delta.bytesSent,
58	    byFrame,
59	    byTarget,
60	    byResultKind,
61	    lastSendAt: delta.timestamp,
62	  };
63	}
64	
65	export interface SendStateContainer {
66	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
67	  setStatus(status: SendServiceStatus): SendStateSnapshot;
68	  addResult(result: SendResult): SendStateSnapshot;
69	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
70	  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot): SendStateSnapshot;
71	}
72	
73	export function createSendState(
74	  initialValue: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot } = {},
75	): SendStateContainer {
76	  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot
77	    ? cloneSendStateSnapshot(initialValue.snapshot)
78	    : emptySnapshot();
79	
80	  return {
81	    getSnapshot() {
82	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
83	    },
84	
85	    setStatus(status) {
86	      snapshot = { ...cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot), status };
87	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
88	    },
89	
90	    addResult(result) {
91	      const cloned = cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
92	      const delta: SendStatsDelta = {
93	        frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,
94	        targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,
95	        resultKind: result.kind,
96	        bytesSent: result.bytesSent,
97	        timestamp: result.timestamp,
98	      };
99	      const recentResults = [...cloned.recentResults, cloneSendResult(result)].slice(-MAX_RECENT_RESULTS);
100	      snapshot = {
101	        ...cloned,
102	        statistics: applyStatsDelta(cloned.statistics, delta),
103	        recentResults,
104	        status: result.kind === 'sent' ? 'idle' : 'error',
105	        ...(result.error ? { lastError: result.error } : {}),
106	      };
107	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
108	    },
109	
110	    resetStats() {
111	      snapshot = { ...cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot), statistics: emptyStatistics() };
112	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
113	    },
114	
115	    resetSnapshot(next = emptySnapshot()) {
116	      snapshot = cloneSendStateSnapshot(next);
117	      return cloneSendStateSnapshot(snapshot);
118	    },
119	  };
120	}
121	
```

1	import {
2	  buildFrame,
3	  validateSendRequest,
4	} from '../core';
5	import type {
6	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
7	  SendBuildIssue,
8	  SendError,
9	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
10	  SendRequest,
11	  SendRequestRef,
12	  SendResult,
13	  SendResultKind,
14	  SendServiceStatus,
15	  SendStateSnapshot,
16	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
17	} from '../core';
18	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
19	import type {
20	  SendFrameReader,
21	  SendResultEmitter,
22	  SendTargetResolver,
23	  SendTransportWriteOutcome,
24	  SendTransportWriter,
25	} from '../adapters';
26	import { createSendState } from '../state';
27	import type { SendStateContainer } from '../state';
28	import {
29	  selectSendResults,
30	  selectSendSnapshot,
31	  selectSendStatistics,
32	  selectSendStatus,
33	} from '../selectors';
34	
35	export interface SendReader {
36	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
37	  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;
38	  listResults(): SendResult[];
39	  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;
40	}
41	
42	export interface SendService extends SendReader {
43	  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
44	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
45	}
46	
47	export interface CreateSendServiceOptions {
48	  readonly frameReader: SendFrameReader;
49	  readonly targetResolver: SendTargetResolver;
50	  readonly transportWriter: SendTransportWriter;
51	  readonly resultEmitter?: SendResultEmitter;
52	  readonly state?: SendStateContainer;
53	  readonly now?: () => string;
54	}
55	
56	function defaultNow(): string {
57	  return new Date().toISOString();
58	}
59	
60	function toRequestRef(request: SendRequest): SendRequestRef {
61	  return {
62	    frameId: request.frameId,
63	    targetId: request.targetId,
64	    context: { ...request.context },
65	  };
66	}
67	
68	function makeResult(
69	  kind: SendResultKind,
70	  requestRef: SendRequestRef,
71	  timestamp: string,
72	  bytesBuilt: number,
73	  bytesSent: number,
74	  error?: SendError,
75	  buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[] = [],
76	): SendResult {
77	  return {
78	    kind,
79	    requestRef,
80	    bytesBuilt,
81	    bytesSent,
82	    timestamp,
83	    ...(error ? { error } : {}),
84	    buildIssues,
85	  };
86	}
87	
88	function frameToBuildInput(
89	  frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset,
90	): { fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[]; totalByteLength: number } {
91	  const fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[] = [];
92	  let offset = 0;
93	  const frameEndian = frame.options?.bigEndian ?? false;
94	
95	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
96	    fields.push({
97	      id: field.id,
98	      dataType: field.dataType,
99	      length: field.length,
100	      bigEndian: field.bigEndian ?? frameEndian,
101	      isASCII: field.isASCII ?? false,
102	      offset,
103	    });
104	    offset += field.length;
105	  }
106	
107	  return { fields, totalByteLength: offset };
108	}
109	
110	export function createSendReader(
111	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
112	): SendReader {
113	  return {
114	    getSnapshot() {
115	      return selectSendSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
116	    },
117	    getStatistics() {
118	      return selectSendStatistics(snapshotProvider());
119	    },
120	    listResults() {
121	      return selectSendResults(snapshotProvider());
122	    },
123	    getStatus() {
124	      return selectSendStatus(snapshotProvider());
125	    },
126	  };
127	}
128	
129	export function createSendService(options: CreateSendServiceOptions): SendService {
130	  const state = options.state ?? createSendState();
131	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
132	  const reader = createSendReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
133	
134	  return {
135	    ...reader,
136	
137	    async execute(request) {
138	      const requestRef = toRequestRef(request);
139	      const timestamp = now();
140	
141	      state.setStatus('sending');
142	
143	      // 1. Validate request
144	      const validationIssues = validateSendRequest(request);
145	      const hasErrors = validationIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');
146	      if (hasErrors) {
147	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, validationIssues);
148	        state.addResult(result);
149	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
150	        return result;
151	      }
152	
153	      // 2. Resolve frame snapshot
154	      const frame = options.frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId);
155	      if (!frame) {
156	        const result = makeResult(
157	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
158	          { kind: 'frame-not-found', message: `Frame not found: ${request.frameId}` },
159	        );
160	        state.addResult(result);
161	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
162	        return result;
163	      }
164	
165	      // 3. Build frame bytes
166	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(frame);
167	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
168	        fields,
169	        totalByteLength,
170	        fieldValues: request.fieldValues,
171	      });
172	
173	      const buildErrors = buildOutput.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
174	      if (buildErrors.length > 0) {
175	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, buildOutput.issues);
176	        state.addResult(result);
177	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
178	        return result;
179	      }
180	
181	      const bytes = [...buildOutput.bytes];
182	
183	      // 4. Resolve target
184	      const target = options.targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId);
185	      if (!target) {
186	        const result = makeResult(
187	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
188	          { kind: 'target-not-found', message: `Target not found: ${request.targetId}` },
189	        );
190	        state.addResult(result);
191	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
192	        return result;
193	      }
194	
195	      if (!target.available) {
196	        const result = makeResult(
197	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
198	          { kind: 'target-not-available', message: `Target not available: ${request.targetId}` },
199	        );
200	        state.addResult(result);
201	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
202	        return result;
203	      }
204	
205	      // 5. Transport write
206	      let writeOutcome: SendTransportWriteOutcome;
207	      try {
208	        writeOutcome = await options.transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytes);
209	      } catch (err) {
210	        const result = makeResult(
211	          'transport-error', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
212	          { kind: 'write-exception', message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown write error' },
213	          buildOutput.issues,
214	        );
215	        state.addResult(result);
216	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
217	        return result;
218	      }
219	
220	      if (!writeOutcome.ok) {
221	        const result = makeResult(
222	          writeOutcome.error?.kind === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'transport-error',
223	          requestRef,
224	          timestamp,
225	          bytes.length,
226	          writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
227	          writeOutcome.error,
228	          buildOutput.issues,
229	        );
230	        state.addResult(result);
231	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
232	        return result;
233	      }
234	
235	      // 6. Success
236	      const result = makeResult(
237	        'sent',
238	        requestRef,
239	        timestamp,
240	        bytes.length,
241	        writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
242	        undefined,
243	        buildOutput.issues,
244	      );
245	      state.addResult(result);
246	      options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
247	      return result;
248	    },
249	
250	    resetStats() {
251	      return state.resetStats();
252	    },
253	  };
254	}
255	
```

1	export const SEND_SOURCES = ['user', 'task', 'scoe', 'test'] as const;
2	export type SendSource = (typeof SEND_SOURCES)[number];
3	
4	export const CHECKSUM_KINDS = ['none', 'sum8', 'xor8', 'crc16-modbus'] as const;
5	export type ChecksumKind = (typeof CHECKSUM_KINDS)[number];
6	
7	export const SEND_RESULT_KINDS = [
8	  'sent',
9	  'build-error',
10	  'target-unavailable',
11	  'transport-error',
12	  'timeout',
13	  'cancelled',
14	] as const;
15	export type SendResultKind = (typeof SEND_RESULT_KINDS)[number];
16	
17	export type SendFieldValue = string | number | boolean;
18	
19	export type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
20	
21	// --- Context ---

27	}
28	
29	// --- Request ---

43	}
44	
45	export interface SendOptions {

48	}
49	
50	// --- Encoding input (service translates frame snapshot to this) ---
51

53	  readonly id: string;

55	  readonly length: number;
56	  readonly bigEndian: boolean;

58	  readonly offset: number;
59	}

66	
67	// --- Build output ---
68	
69	export interface ResolvedFieldValue {

89	
90	// --- Result ---
91	
92	export interface SendError {

94	  readonly message: string;
95	}
96	
97	export interface SendResult {

100	  readonly bytesBuilt: number;
101	  readonly bytesSent: number;

103	  readonly error?: SendError;

105	}
106	
107	// --- Statistics ---
108

112	}
113

121	  readonly byResultKind: Readonly<Partial<Record<SendResultKind, number>>>;
122	  readonly lastSendAt?: string;
123	}
124	
125	export interface SendStatsDelta {

132	
133	// --- State ---

142	}
143	
144	export type ReadonlySendStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<SendStateSnapshot>;

100	const INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT = '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';
101	
102	function createDefaultFrameReader(): FrameAssetReader {
103	  return createFrameAssetReader(() => ({
104	    frames: [],
105	  }));

108	function outcomeIsOk(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): boolean {
109	  return outcome.kind === 'matched' || outcome.kind === 'unmatched';
110	}

113	  snapshot: ReceiveStateSnapshot,
114	  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[] = [],
115	  reference?: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot,
116	): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
117	  const issues = outcomes.flatMap((item) => item.issues.map(cloneIssue));
118	  return {
119	    ok: outcomes.every(outcomeIsOk),
120	    outcomes: outcomes.map(cloneBatchOutcome),

124	  };

157	    },

161	    },
162	  };
163	}
164	
165	export function createReceiveService(
166	  options: CreateReceiveServiceOptions = {},
167	): ReceiveService {
168	  const state = options.state ?? createReceiveState();
169	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
170	  const frameReader = options.frameReader ?? createDefaultFrameReader();
171	  const reader = createReceiveReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
172	  let reference: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot = {
173	    frames: [],
174	    referenceVersion: state.getSnapshot().referenceVersion,
175	    refreshedAt: INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT,
176	  };
177	
178	  function apply(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
179	    const snapshot = state.applyOutcome(outcome);
180	    return serviceOutcome(snapshot, [outcome]);
181	  }
182	
183	  function refresh(frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[]): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
184	    reference = {
185	      frames: cloneFrameReferences(frames),
186	      referenceVersion: reference.referenceVersion + 1,
187	      refreshedAt: now(),
188	    };
189	    const snapshot = state.setReferenceVersion(reference.referenceVersion);
190	    return serviceOutcome(snapshot, [], reference);
191	  }
192	
193	  function ingest(batch: ReceiveInputBatch): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
194	    if (
195	      batch.referenceVersion !== undefined &&
196	      batch.referenceVersion !== reference.referenceVersion
197	    ) {
198	      return apply(
199	        createStaleReceiveInputOutcome(
200	          batch.id,
201	          batch,
202	          reference.referenceVersion,
203	          now(),
204	        ),
205	      );
206	    }
207	
208	    return apply(
209	      processReceiveBatch({
210	        batch,
211	        reference,
212	        processedAt: now(),
213	      }),
214	    );
215	  }
216	
217	  function recordInputError(error: ReceiveInputError): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
218	    return apply(createReceiveInputErrorOutcome(error, now()));
219	  }
220	
221	  return {
222	    ...reader,
223	
224	    refreshFrameReferences(frames) {
225	      return refresh(frames ?? frameReader.findFrames({ direction: 'receive' }));
226	    },
227	
228	    ingestBatch: ingest,
229	
230	    recordInputError,
231	
232	    async drainInputSource(source) {
233	      const outcomes: ReceiveBatchOutcome[] = [];
234	      for (const event of await source.drainEvents()) {
235	        const serviceCall = event.kind === 'batch' ? ingest(event.batch) : recordInputError(event.error);
236	        outcomes.push(...serviceCall.outcomes);
237	      }
238	
239	      return serviceOutcome(state.getSnapshot(), outcomes);
240	    },
241	
242	    reset(scope = 'all') {
243	      const snapshot = state.reset(scope);
244	      if (scope === 'all') {
245	        reference = {
246	          frames: [],
247	          referenceVersion: 0,
248	          refreshedAt: INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT,
249	        };
250	      }
251	      return serviceOutcome(snapshot);
252	    },
253	  };
254	}
255

1	import type { TransportTargetSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
2	import type { SendTargetResolver, SendTransportWriteOutcome, SendTransportWriter } from './ports';
3	
4	export interface FakeConnectionWriterOptions {
5	  readonly targets?: readonly TransportTargetSnapshot[];
6	  readonly defaultWriteOutcome?: SendTransportWriteOutcome;
7	}
8	
9	export interface RecordedWrite {
10	  readonly connectionId: string;
11	  readonly bytes: readonly number[];
12	}
13	
14	export function createFakeConnectionWriter(
15	  options: FakeConnectionWriterOptions = {},
16	): SendTargetResolver & SendTransportWriter & { readonly recordedWrites: readonly RecordedWrite[] } {
17	  const targets = new Map<string, TransportTargetSnapshot>();
18	  for (const t of options.targets ?? []) {
19	    targets.set(t.targetId, t);
20	  }
21	
22	  const recordedWrites: RecordedWrite[] = [];
23	  const defaultOutcome = options.defaultWriteOutcome ?? { ok: true, bytesWritten: 0 };
24	
25	  return {
26	    get recordedWrites() {
27	      return recordedWrites;
28	    },
29	
30	    resolveTarget(targetId: string) {
31	      return targets.get(targetId);
32	    },
33	
34	    async writeBytes(connectionId: string, bytes: readonly number[]): Promise<SendTransportWriteOutcome> {
35	      recordedWrites.push({ connectionId, bytes: [...bytes] });
36	      return { ...defaultOutcome, bytesWritten: bytes.length };
37	    },
38	  };
39	}
40	
41	export function createFailingWriter(errorKind: string, errorMessage: string): SendTransportWriter {
42	  return {
43	    async writeBytes(): Promise<SendTransportWriteOutcome> {
44	      return { ok: false, bytesWritten: 0, error: { kind: errorKind, message: errorMessage } };
45	    },
46	  };
47	}
48

```
1	/**
2	 * Recursively makes all properties of T readonly, including nested arrays and objects.
3	 * Shared across all features to avoid duplicate definitions.
4	 */
5	export type ReadonlyDeep<T> = T extends readonly (infer Item)[]
6	  ? readonly ReadonlyDeep<Item>[]
7	  : T extends object
8	    ? { readonly [Key in keyof T]: ReadonlyDeep<T[Key]> }
9	    : T;
10

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/state/task-state.ts",
  "content": "import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';\nimport type {\n  TaskDefinition,\n  TaskInstanceState,\n  TaskStepResult,\n} from '../core';\nimport { cloneInstanceState, cloneStepResult } from '../core';\n\nconst MAX_HISTORY = 50;\nconst DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS = 100;\n\n// --- Statistics ---\n\nexport interface TaskStatisticsSnapshot {\n  readonly totalCreated: number;\n  readonly totalCompleted: number;\n  readonly totalStopped: number;\n  readonly totalFailed: number;\n  readonly totalStepsExecuted: number;\n  readonly totalStepsSucceeded: number;\n  readonly totalStepsFailed: number;\n  readonly totalStepsSkipped: number;\n}\n\n// --- State snapshot ---\n\nexport interface TaskStateSnapshot {\n  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport type ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<TaskStateSnapshot>;\n\n// --- Container ---\n\nexport interface TaskStateContainer {\n  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;\n  createInstance(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;\n  updateInstance(\n    instanceId: string,\n    updates: Partial<Omit<TaskInstanceState, 'instanceId' | 'definitionRef'>>,\n  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  removeInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  addStepResult(instanceId: string, result: TaskStepResult): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  moveToHistory(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  resetStats(): TaskStateSnapshot;\n}\n\n// --- Helpers ---\n\nfunction emptyStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {\n  return {\n    totalCreated: 0,\n    totalCompleted: 0,\n    totalStopped: 0,\n    totalFailed: 0,\n    totalStepsExecuted: 0,\n    totalStepsSucceeded: 0,\n    totalStepsFailed: 0,\n    totalStepsSkipped: 0,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {\n  switch (result.kind) {\n    case 'send':\n      return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';\n    case 'wait-condition':\n      return !result.matched && result.timedOut;\n    case 'delay':\n      return !result.completed;\n  }\n}\n\n// --- Factory ---\n\nexport function createTaskState(\n  initialValue?: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot },\n): TaskStateContainer {\n  let instances = new Map<string, TaskInstanceState>();\n  let history: TaskInstanceState[] = [];\n  let statistics = emptyStatistics();\n\n  if (initialValue?.snapshot) {\n    for (const inst of initialValue.snapshot.instances) {\n      instances.set(inst.instanceId, cloneInstanceState(inst));\n    }\n    history = initialValue.snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState);\n    statistics = { ...initialValue.snapshot.statistics };\n  }\n\n  function getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot {\n    return {\n      instances: [...instances.values()].map(cloneInstanceState),\n      history: history.map(cloneInstanceState),\n      statistics: { ...statistics },\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    getSnapshot,\n\n    createInstance(instanceId, definition) {\n      const instance: TaskInstanceState = {\n        instanceId,\n        definitionRef: definition,\n        lifecycle: 'created',\n        currentStepIndex: 0,\n        currentIteration: 0,\n        stepResults: [],\n      };\n      instances.set(instanceId, instance);\n      statistics = { ...statistics, totalCreated: statistics.totalCreated + 1 };\n      return cloneInstanceState(instance);\n    },\n\n    updateInstance(instanceId, updates) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {\n        ...existing,\n        ...updates,\n        instanceId: existing.instanceId,\n        definitionRef: existing.definitionRef,\n      };\n      instances.set(instanceId, updated);\n\n      if (updates.lifecycle === 'completed') {\n        statistics = { ...statistics, totalCompleted: statistics.totalCompleted + 1 };\n      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'stopped') {\n        statistics = { ...statistics, totalStopped: statistics.totalStopped + 1 };\n      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'failed') {\n        statistics = { ...statistics, totalFailed: statistics.totalFailed + 1 };\n      }\n\n      return cloneInstanceState(updated);\n    },\n\n    removeInstance(instanceId) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n      instances.delete(instanceId);\n      return cloneInstanceState(existing);\n    },\n\n    addStepResult(instanceId, result) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n\n      const newResults = [...existing.stepResults, cloneStepResult(result)];\n      const maxIter = Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration);\n      const minIteration = Math.max(0, maxIter - DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS + 1);\n      const boundedResults = newResults.filter((r) => r.iteration >= minIteration);\n\n      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {\n        ...existing,\n        stepResults: boundedResults,\n        currentStepIndex: result.stepIndex,\n        currentIteration: Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration),\n      };\n      instances.set(instanceId, updated);\n\n      const isSkipped = result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step';\n      const isFailed = !isSkipped && isStepResultFailed(result);\n      statistics = {\n        ...statistics,\n        totalStepsExecuted: statistics.totalStepsExecuted + 1,\n        totalStepsSucceeded: statistics.totalStepsSucceeded + (isSkipped || isFailed ? 0 : 1),\n        totalStepsFailed: statistics.totalStepsFailed + (isFailed ? 1 : 0),\n        totalStepsSkipped: statistics.totalStepsSkipped + (isSkipped ? 1 : 0),\n      };\n\n      return cloneInstanceState(updated);\n    },\n\n    getInstance(instanceId) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      return existing ? cloneInstanceState(existing) : undefined;\n    },\n\n    moveToHistory(instanceId) {\n      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);\n      if (!existing) return undefined;\n      instances.delete(instanceId);\n      history = [...history, cloneInstanceState(existing)].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);\n      return cloneInstanceState(existing);\n    },\n\n    resetStats() {\n      statistics = emptyStatistics();\n      return getSnapshot();\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { SendRequest, SendResult } from '@/features/send';\nimport type { SendServiceProvider } from './ports';\n\nexport interface FakeSendServiceOptions {\n  readonly results?: readonly SendResult[];\n  readonly delayMs?: number;\n}\n\nexport interface RecordedCall {\n  readonly request: SendRequest;\n  readonly index: number;\n}\n\nfunction defaultSentResult(): SendResult {\n  return {\n    kind: 'sent',\n    requestRef: { frameId: 'frame-1', targetId: 'target-1', context: { source: 'task' } },\n    bytesBuilt: 10,\n    bytesSent: 10,\n    timestamp: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',\n    buildIssues: [],\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createFakeSendService(\n  options: FakeSendServiceOptions = {},\n): SendServiceProvider & { readonly calls: readonly RecordedCall[] } {\n  const calls: RecordedCall[] = [];\n  const results = options.results ?? [defaultSentResult()];\n  const delayMs = options.delayMs ?? 0;\n  let callIndex = 0;\n\n  return {\n    get calls() {\n      return calls;\n    },\n\n    async execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult> {\n      const index = callIndex;\n      calls.push({ request, index });\n      callIndex++;\n      if (delayMs > 0) {\n        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs));\n      }\n      const result = index < results.length ? results[index] : results[results.length - 1];\n      return { ...result };\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createFailingSendService(\n  errorKind: string,\n  errorMessage: string,\n): SendServiceProvider {\n  return {\n    async execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult> {\n      return {\n        kind: 'transport-error',\n        requestRef: {\n          frameId: request.frameId,\n          targetId: request.targetId,\n          context: { ...request.context },\n        },\n        bytesBuilt: 0,\n        bytesSent: 0,\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n        error: { kind: errorKind, message: errorMessage },\n        buildIssues: [],\n      };\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';\nimport type { ReceiveEventSource } from './ports';\n\nexport interface FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions {\n  readonly autoEmit?: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];\n  readonly autoEmitIntervalMs?: number;\n}\n\nexport function createFakeReceiveEventSource(\n  _options?: FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions,\n): ReceiveEventSource & {\n  emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void;\n  readonly emitted: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];\n} {\n  const emitted: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];\n  const handlers = new Set<(input: ConditionMatchInput) => void>();\n\n  return {\n    get emitted() {\n      return emitted;\n    },\n\n    emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {\n      emitted.push(input);\n      for (const handler of handlers) {\n        handler(input);\n      }\n    },\n\n    subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void {\n      handlers.add(handler);\n      return () => {\n        handlers.delete(handler);\n      };\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type {\n  TaskInstanceState,\n  TaskProgress,\n} from '../core';\nimport { calculateProgress, cloneInstanceState } from '../core';\nimport type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';\n\nexport function selectTaskInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {\n  return snapshot.instances.map(cloneInstanceState);\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskInstance(\n  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,\n  instanceId: string,\n): TaskInstanceState | undefined {\n  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);\n  return instance ? cloneInstanceState(instance) : undefined;\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskProgress(\n  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,\n  instanceId: string,\n): TaskProgress | undefined {\n  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);\n  return instance ? calculateProgress(instance) : undefined;\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskHistory(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {\n  return snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState);\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskStatistics(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {\n  return { ...snapshot.statistics };\n}\n\nexport interface TaskUiSnapshot {\n  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];\n  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport function selectTaskSnapshot(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskUiSnapshot {\n  return {\n    instances: snapshot.instances.map(cloneInstanceState),\n    history: snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState),\n    statistics: { ...snapshot.statistics },\n  };\n}\n"

ConditionMatchInput,\n} from '../core';\nimport {\n  transition,\n  isTerminal,\n  evaluateCondition,\n  cloneInstanceState,\n} from '../core';\nimport type { SendServiceProvider, ReceiveEventSource } from '../adapters';\nimport type { TaskStateContainer, TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';\nimport { createTaskState } from '../state';\nimport {\n  selectTaskInstance,\n  selectTaskProgress,\n  selectTaskSnapshot,\n  selectTaskStatistics,\n  selectTaskInstances,\n  selectTaskHistory,\n} from '../selectors';\n\n// --- Public interfaces ---\n\nexport interface TaskReader {\n  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;\n  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;\n  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined;\n  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskService extends TaskReader {\n  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;\n  startTask(instanceId: string): void;\n  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;\n  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;\n  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;\n  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;\n  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;\n}\n\nexport interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {\n  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;\n  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;\n  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;\n  readonly now?: () => string;\n}\n\n// --- Condition registry ---\n\ninterface ConditionEntry {\n  readonly condition: WaitCondition;\n  readonly onMatch: (value: number | string) => void;\n}\n\nclass ConditionRegistry {\n  private entries = new Map<string, Set<ConditionEntry>>();\n\n  register(condition: WaitCondition, onMatch: (value: number | string) => void): ConditionEntry {\n    const entry: ConditionEntry = { condition, onMatch };\n    const key = condition.frameId;\n    if (!this.entries.has(key)) {\n      this.entries.set(key, new Set());\n    }\n    this.entries.get(key)!.add(entry);\n    return entry;\n  }\n\n  unregister(entry: ConditionEntry): void {\n    const key = entry.condition.frameId;\n    const set = this.entries.get(key);\n    if (set) {\n      set.delete(entry);\n      if (set.size === 0) {\n        this.entries.delete(key);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {\n    const set = this.entries.get(input.frameId);\n    if (!set) return;\n    for (const entry of set) {\n      if (evaluateCondition(entry.condition, input)) {\n        entry.onMatch(input.value as number | string);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  clear(): void {\n    this.entries.clear();\n  }\n\n  get size(): number {\n    let total = 0;\n    for (const set of this.entries.values()) {\n      total += set.size;\n    }\n    return total;\n  }\n}\n\n// --- Helpers ---\n\nfunction sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));\n}\n\nfunction buildSendRequest(\n  stepConfig: TaskDefinition['steps'][number] & { kind: 'send' },\n  definition: TaskDefinition,\n  stepIndex: number,\n): SendRequest {\n  return {\n    frameId: stepConfig.sendConfig.frameId,\n    fieldValues: stepConfig.sendConfig.fieldValues,\n    targetId: stepConfig.sendConfig.targetId ?? definition.targetId ?? '',\n    options: stepConfig.sendConfig.options ?? {},\n    context: { source: 'task', taskId: definition.id, stepIndex },\n  };\n}\n\n// --- Factory ---\n\nlet nextInstanceId = 1;\n\nexport function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService {\n  const sendService = options.sendService;\n  const receiveSource = options.receiveEventSource;\n  const state = options.state ?? createTaskState();\n  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());\n\n  // Execution control\n  const abortResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();\n  const settleResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();\n\n  // Condition matching\n  const conditionRegistry = new ConditionRegistry();\n  let receiveSubscription: (() => void) | null = null;\n  let subscriptionRefCount = 0;\n\n  // --- Subscription management ---\n\n  function ensureSubscription(): void {\n    if (receiveSubscription) {\n      subscriptionRefCount++;\n      return;\n    }\n    receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {\n      conditionRegistry.processInput(input);\n    });\n    subscriptionRefCount = 1;\n  }\n\n  function releaseSubscription(): void {\n    subscriptionRefCount--;\n    if (subscriptionRefCount <= 0 && receiveSubscription) {\n      receiveSubscription();\n      receiveSubscription = null;\n      subscriptionRefCount = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // --- Abort / settle ---\n\n  function createAbortSignal(instanceId: string): Promise<void> {\n    return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n      abortResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);\n    });\n  }\n\n  function abortInstance(instanceId: string): void {\n    const resolve = abortResolvers.get(instanceId);\n    if (resolve) {\n      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);\n      resolve();\n    }\n  }\n\n  function resolveSettle(instanceId: string): void {\n    const resolve = settleResolvers.get(instanceId);\n    if (resolve) {\n      settleResolvers.delete(instanceId);\n      resolve();\n    }\n  }\n\n  // --- Lifecycle helpers ---\n\n  function updateLifecycle(\n    instanceId: string,\n    action: 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail',\n    extra?: Partial<TaskInstanceState>,\n  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined {\n    const instance = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n    if (!instance) return undefined;\n    const newStatus = transition(instance.lifecycle, action);\n    const timestampField =\n      action === 'start' ? 'startedAt' :\n      action === 'pause' ? 'pausedAt' :\n      action === 'stop' ? 'stoppedAt' :\n      action === 'complete' ? 'completedAt' :\n      action === 'fail' ? 'failedAt' : undefined;\n    return state.updateInstance(instanceId, {\n      lifecycle: newStatus,\n      ...(timestampField ? { [timestampField]: now() } : {}),\n      ...extra,\n    });\n  }\n\n  function completeTask(instanceId: string): void {\n    updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'complete');\n    conditionRegistry.clear();\n    resolveSettle(instanceId);\n  }\n\n  function failTask(instanceId: string, error: string): void {\n    updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error });\n    conditionRegistry.clear();\n    resolveSettle(instanceId);\n  }\n\n  // --- Step execution ---\n\n  async function executeSendStep(\n    instanceId: string,\n    step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'send' }>,\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n  ): Promise<TaskStepResult> {\n    const request = buildSendRequest(step, definition, stepIndex);\n    const sendResult = await sendService.execute(request);\n    return { kind: 'send', stepIndex, iteration, sendResult };\n  }\n\n  async function executeWaitConditionStep(\n    instanceId: string,\n    step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'wait-condition' }>,\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; interrupted: boolean }> {\n    const { condition, timeoutMs } = step.waitConfig;\n\n    return new Promise((resolve) => {\n      let settled = false;\n\n      const entry = conditionRegistry.register(condition, (value) => {\n        if (settled) return;\n        settled = true;\n        conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);\n        resolve({\n          result: {\n            kind: 'wait-condition',\n            stepIndex,\n            iteration,\n            matched: true,\n            matchedValue: value,\n            timedOut: false,\n          },\n          interrupted: false,\n        });\n      });\n\n      const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n        if (settled) return;\n        settled = true;\n        conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);\n        resolve({\n          result: {\n            kind: 'wait-condition',\n            stepIndex,\n            iteration,\n            matched: false,\n            timedOut: true,\n          },\n          interrupted: false,\n        });\n      }, timeoutMs);\n\n      signal.then(() => {\n        if (settled) return;\n        settled = true;\n        clearTimeout(timer);\n        conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);\n        resolve({\n          result: {\n            kind: 'wait-condition',\n            stepIndex,\n            iteration,\n            matched: false,\n            timedOut: false,\n          },\n          interrupted: true,\n        });\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  async function executeDelayStep(\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    durationMs: number,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; interrupted: boolean }> {\n    const race = await Promise.race([\n      sleep(durationMs).then(() => 'done' as const),\n      signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),\n    ]);\n    return {\n      result: { kind: 'delay', stepIndex, iteration, completed: race === 'done' },\n      interrupted: race === 'interrupted',\n    };\n  }\n\n  async function applyErrorPolicy(\n    instanceId: string,\n    failedResult: TaskStepResult,\n    policy: TaskDefinition['errorPolicy'],\n    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {\n    switch (policy.onFailure) {\n      case 'stop':\n        updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');\n        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'stop' }, shouldStop: true };\n\n      case 'pause':\n        updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');\n        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'pause' }, shouldStop: true };\n\n      case 'skip-step':\n        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };\n\n      case 'retry': {\n        const maxRetries = policy.retryCount ?? 0;\n        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {\n          if (policy.retryDelayMs && policy.retryDelayMs > 0) {\n            const race = await Promise.race([\n              sleep(policy.retryDelayMs).then(() => false),\n              signal.then(() => true),\n            ]);\n            if (race) return null;\n          }\n\n          const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n          if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return null;\n\n          const retryResult = await executeStepCore(\n            instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal,\n          );\n          if (retryResult === null) return null;\n          if (!isStepFailed(retryResult.result)) return retryResult;\n        }\n        // Exhausted retries\n        updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error: 'Retry exhausted' });\n        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'retry' }, shouldStop: true };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function executeStepCore(\n    instanceId: string,\n    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {\n    switch (step.kind) {\n      case 'send': {\n        const result = await executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);\n        if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {\n          return applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);\n        }\n        return { result, shouldStop: false };\n      }\n\n      case 'wait-condition': {\n        const { result, interrupted } = await executeWaitConditionStep(\n          instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,\n        );\n        if (interrupted) return null;\n\n        if (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {\n          switch (step.waitConfig.onTimeout) {\n            case 'continue':\n              return { result, shouldStop: false };\n            case 'skip':\n              return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };\n            case 'fail':\n              return applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);\n          }\n        }\n        return { result, shouldStop: false };\n      }\n\n      case 'delay': {\n        const { result, interrupted } = await executeDelayStep(\n          iteration, stepIndex, step.delayConfig.durationMs, signal,\n        );\n        if (interrupted) return null;\n        return { result, shouldStop: false };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  function isStepFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {\n    switch (result.kind) {\n      case 'send': return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';\n      case 'wait-condition': return !result.matched && result.timedOut;\n      case 'delay': return !result.completed;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // --- executeSteps ---\n\n  async function executeSteps(\n    instanceId: string,\n    iteration: number,\n    steps: readonly TaskDefinition['steps'],\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<boolean> {\n    // Determine start index: skip already-completed steps in this iteration\n    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n    if (!inst) return false;\n    const completedInIter = inst.stepResults\n      .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)\n      .map((r) => r.stepIndex);\n    const startIndex = completedInIter.length;\n\n    for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {\n      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;\n\n      state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });\n\n      const outcome = await executeStepCore(\n        instanceId, steps[i], iteration, i, definition, signal,\n      );\n\n      if (outcome === null) return false;\n\n      state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);\n\n      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  // --- Scheduling loops ---\n\n  async function runTimedLoop(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const { intervalMs, maxCount, delayBeforeStartMs } = definition;\n    const maxIter = maxCount ?? Infinity;\n    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n    const startIteration = inst?.currentIteration ?? 0;\n\n    if (delayBeforeStartMs && delayBeforeStartMs > 0 && startIteration === 0) {\n      const race = await Promise.race([\n        sleep(delayBeforeStartMs).then(() => 'done' as const),\n        signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),\n      ]);\n      if (race === 'interrupted') return;\n    }\n\n    for (let iter = startIteration; iter < maxIter; iter++) {\n      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return;\n\n      const completed = await executeSteps(\n        instanceId, iter, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n      );\n\n      if (!completed) return;\n\n      const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') return;\n\n      // Not last iteration → wait for interval\n      if (iter + 1 < maxIter) {\n        const race = await Promise.race([\n          sleep(intervalMs ?? 1000).then(() => 'done' as const),\n          signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),\n        ]);\n        if (race === 'interrupted') return;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function runTriggerLoop(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const triggerCondition = definition.triggerCondition;\n    if (!triggerCondition) return;\n\n    const maxTriggerCount = definition.maxTriggerCount ?? Infinity;\n    const cooldownMs = definition.cooldownMs ?? 0;\n    let triggerCount = 0;\n    let lastTriggerTime = 0;\n    let nextTriggerResolve: ((value: number | string) => void) | null = null;\n\n    const handler = (input: ConditionMatchInput) => {\n      if (!evaluateCondition(triggerCondition, input)) return;\n      const currentTime = Date.now();\n      if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && currentTime - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;\n      if (triggerCount >= maxTriggerCount) return;\n\n      lastTriggerTime = currentTime;\n      if (nextTriggerResolve) {\n        nextTriggerResolve(input.value as number | string);\n        nextTriggerResolve = null;\n      }\n    };\n\n    // Use condition registry for trigger matching\n    const entry = conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler);\n\n    try {\n      while (triggerCount < maxTriggerCount) {\n        const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n        if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') break;\n\n        const triggerValue = await Promise.race([\n          new Promise<number | string>((resolve) => {\n            nextTriggerResolve = resolve;\n          }),\n          signal.then(() => null as number | string | null),\n        ]);\n\n        if (triggerValue === null) break;\n\n        triggerCount++;\n\n        await executeSteps(\n          instanceId, triggerCount - 1, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n        );\n\n        const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n        if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') break;\n      }\n    } finally {\n      conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function runSequenceLoop(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    await executeSteps(instanceId, 0, definition.steps, definition, signal);\n  }\n\n  async function runExecutionLoop(\n    instanceId: string,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n    if (!inst) return;\n\n    try {\n      switch (inst.definitionRef.schedulingMode) {\n        case 'timed':\n          await runTimedLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);\n          break;\n        case 'trigger':\n          ensureSubscription();\n          await runTriggerLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);\n          break;\n        case 'sequence':\n          await runSequenceLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);\n          break;\n      }\n\n      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {\n        completeTask(instanceId);\n      }\n    } catch (err) {\n      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {\n        failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n      }\n    } finally {\n      releaseSubscription();\n      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // --- Public API ---\n\n  return {\n    getSnapshot() {\n      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());\n    },\n\n    getInstance(instanceId) {\n      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);\n    },\n\n    getProgress(instanceId) {\n      return selectTaskProgress(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);\n    },\n\n    getStatistics() {\n      return selectTaskStatistics(state.getSnapshot());\n    },\n\n    createTask(definition) {\n      const instanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;\n      return state.createInstance(instanceId, definition);\n    },\n\n    startTask(instanceId) {\n      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return;\n\n      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'start');\n\n      const signal = createAbortSignal(instanceId);\n\n      // Fire-and-forget execution loop\n      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);\n    },\n\n    pauseTask(instanceId) {\n      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return;\n\n      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');\n      conditionRegistry.clear();\n      abortInstance(instanceId);\n    },\n\n    resumeTask(instanceId) {\n      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;\n\n      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'resume');\n\n      const signal = createAbortSignal(instanceId);\n      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);\n    },\n\n    stopTask(instanceId) {\n      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst) return;\n      if (inst.lifecycle !== 'running' && inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;\n\n      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');\n      conditionRegistry.clear();\n      abortInstance(instanceId);\n    },\n\n    removeTask(instanceId) {\n      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst) return;\n      if (!isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;\n\n      abortInstance(instanceId);\n      state.removeInstance(instanceId);\n    },\n\n    async onSettled(instanceId) {\n      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;\n\n      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n        settleResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);\n      });\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

}\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));\n  }\n}\n\n// ========================================================================\n// Timed task\n// Infinity;\n    const cooldownMs = definition.cooldownMs ?? 0;\n    let triggerCount = 0;\n    let lastTriggerTime = 0;\n    let nextTriggerResolve: ((value: number | string) => void) | null = null;\n\n    const handler = (input: ConditionMatchInput) => {\n      if (!evaluateCondition(triggerCondition, input)) return;\n      const currentTime = Date.now();\n      if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && currentTime - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;\n      if (triggerCount >= maxTriggerCount) return;\n\n      lastTriggerTime = currentTime;\n      if (nextTriggerResolve) {\n        nextTriggerResolve(input.value as number | string);\n        nextTriggerResolve = null;\n      }\n    };\n\n    // Use condition registry for trigger matching\n    const entry = conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler);\n\n    try {\n      while (triggerCount < maxTriggerCount) {\n        const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n        if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') break;\n\n        const triggerValue = await Promise.race([\n          new Promise<number | string>((resolve) => {\n            nextTriggerResolve = resolve;\n          }),\n          signal.then(() => null as number | string | null),\n        ]);\n\n        if (triggerValue === null) break;\n\n        triggerCount++;\n\n        await executeSteps(\n          instanceId, triggerCount - 1, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n        );\n\n        const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n        if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') break;\n      }\n    } finally {\n      conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);\n    }\n  }",
  "new_string": "  async function runTriggerLoop(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const triggerCondition = definition.triggerCondition;\n    if (!triggerCondition) return;\n\n    const maxTriggerCount = definition.maxTriggerCount ?? Infinity;\n    const cooldownMs = definition.cooldownMs ?? 0;\n    let triggerCount = 0;\n    let lastTriggerTime = 0;\n    const triggerQueue: (number | string)[] = [];\n    let queueNotify: (() => void) | null = null;\n\n    const handler = (input: ConditionMatchInput) => {\n      if (!evaluateCondition(triggerCondition, input)) return;\n      const currentTime = Date.now();\n      if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && currentTime - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;\n      if (triggerCount + triggerQueue.length >= maxTriggerCount) return;\n\n      lastTriggerTime = currentTime;\n      triggerQueue.push(input.value as number | string);\n      if (queueNotify) {\n        queueNotify();\n        queueNotify = null;\n      }\n    };\n\n    const entry = conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler);\n\n    try {\n      while (triggerCount < maxTriggerCount) {\n        const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n        if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') break;\n\n        // Wait for queued trigger\n        if (triggerQueue.length === 0) {\n          const notified = await Promise.race([\n            new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {\n              queueNotify = () => resolve(true);\n            }),\n            signal.then(() => false),\n          ]);\n          if (!notified) break;\n        }\n\n        triggerQueue.shift();\n        triggerCount++;\n\n        await executeSteps(\n          instanceId, triggerCount - 1, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n        );\n\n        const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);\n        if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') break;\n      }\n    } finally {\n      conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);\n    }\n  }"

523	  async function runTriggerLoop(
524	    instanceId: string,
525	    definition: TaskDefinition,
526	    signal: Promise<void>,
527	  ): Promise<void> {
528	    const triggerCondition = definition.triggerCondition;
529	    if (!triggerCondition) return;
530	
531	    const maxTriggerCount = definition.maxTriggerCount ?? Infinity;
532	    const cooldownMs = definition.cooldownMs ?? 0;
533	    let triggerCount = 0;
534	    let lastTriggerTime = 0;
535	    const triggerQueue: (number | string)[] = [];
536	    let queueNotify: (() => void) | null = null;
537	
538	    const handler = (input: ConditionMatchInput) => {
539	      if (!evaluateCondition(triggerCondition, input)) return;
540	      const currentTime = Date.now();
541	      if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && currentTime - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
542	      if (triggerCount + triggerQueue.length >= maxTriggerCount) return;
543	
544	      lastTriggerTime = currentTime;
545	      triggerQueue.push(input.value as number | string);
546	      if (queueNotify) {
547	        queueNotify();
548	        queueNotify = null;
549	      }
550	    };
551	
552	    const entry = conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler);
553	
554	    try {
555	      while (triggerCount < maxTriggerCount) {
556	        const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
557	        if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') break;
558	
559	        // Wait for queued trigger
560	        if (triggerQueue.length === 0) {
561	          const notified = await Promise.race([
562	            new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
563	              queueNotify = () => resolve(true);
564	            }),
565	            signal.then(() => false),
566	          ]);
567	          if (!notified) break;
568	        }
569	
570	        triggerQueue.shift();
571	        triggerCount++;
572	
573	        await executeSteps(
574	          instanceId, triggerCount - 1, definition.steps, definition, signal,
575	        );
576	
577	        const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);
578	        if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') break;
579	      }
580	    } finally {
581	      conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);
582	    }
583	  }
584	
585	  async function runSequenceLoop(
586	    instanceId: string,
587	    definition: TaskDefinition,
588	    signal: Promise<void>,
589	  ): Promise<void> {
590	    await executeSteps(instanceId, 0, definition.steps, definition, signal);
591	  }
592	
593	  async function runExecutionLoop(
594	    instanceId: string,
595	    signal: Promise<void>,
596	  ): Promise<void> {
597	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
598	    if (!inst) return;
599	
600	    try {
601	      ensureSubscription();
602	      switch (inst.definitionRef.schedulingMode) {
603	        case 'timed':
604	          await runTimedLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
605	          break;
606	        case 'trigger':
607	          await runTriggerLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
608	          break;
609	        case 'sequence':
610	          await runSequenceLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
611	          break;
612	      }
613	
614	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
615	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
616	        completeTask(instanceId);
617	      }
618	    } catch (err) {
619	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
620	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
621	        failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
622	      }
623	    } finally {
624	      releaseSubscription();
625	      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
626	    }
627	  }
628

630

633	      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());
634	    },
635	
636	    getInstance(instanceId) {
637	      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
638	    },

1	import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
2	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';

11	// --- Helpers ---
12	
13	function makeSentResult(): SendResult {
14	  return makeSendResult();
15	}
16	
17	function makeErrorResult(kind: 'transport-error' | 'timeout' | 'target-unavailable' | 'build-error' = 'transport-error'): SendResult {
18	  return makeSendResult({ kind }) as SendResult;
19	}

21	function createTestSetup(overrides: { definition?: TaskDefinition; sendResults?: readonly SendResult[] } = {}) {
22	  const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results: overrides.sendResults ?? [makeSentResult()] });
23	  const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();
24	  const service = createTaskService({
25	    sendService: fakeSend,
26	    receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,
27	    now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',
28	  });
29	  const definition = overrides.definition ?? timedTaskDef();
30	  const instance = service.createTask(definition);
31	  return { service, fakeSend, fakeReceive, definition, instance };
32	}
33	
34	async function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<void> {
35	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
36	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
37	    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);
38	    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
39	      return;
40	    }
41	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
42	  }
43	}

49	describe('TaskService - timed scheduling', () => {

69	
70	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
71	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId);
72	
73	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
74	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
75	    const iterations = new Set(final!.stepResults.map((r) => r.iteration));
76	    expect(iterations.size).toBe(maxCount);
77	  });
78	});

83	
84	describe('TaskService - trigger scheduling', () => {
85	  it('fires on matching receive event', async () => {
86	    const def = triggerTaskDef();
87	    const { service, fakeReceive, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({
88	      definition: def,
89	      sendResults: [makeSentResult()],
90	    });
91	
92	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
93	
94	    // Allow subscription to establish
95	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
96	
97	    // Emit matching event
98	    fakeReceive.emit({
99	      frameId: def.triggerCondition!.frameId,
100	      fieldId: def.triggerCondition!.fieldId,
101	      value: def.triggerCondition!.threshold,
102	    });
103	
104	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
105	
106	    // Task should have executed at least one step
107	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
108	  });
109	
110	  it('respects maxTriggerCount', async () => {
111	    const maxTriggerCount = 2;
112	    const def: TaskDefinition = { ...triggerTaskDef(), maxTriggerCount, cooldownMs: 0 };
113	    const results = Array.from({ length: maxTriggerCount + 2 }, () => makeSentResult());
114	    const { service, fakeReceive, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({
115	      definition: def,
116	      sendResults: results,
117	    });
118	
119	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
120	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
121	
122	    for (let i = 0; i < maxTriggerCount + 2; i++) {
123	      fakeReceive.emit({
124	        frameId: def.triggerCondition!.frameId,
125	        fieldId: def.triggerCondition!.fieldId,
126	        value: def.triggerCondition!.threshold,
127	      });
128	      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
129	    }
130	
131	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
132	
133	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(maxTriggerCount * def.steps.length);
134	  });
135	
136	  it('ignores non-matching events', async () => {
137	    const def = triggerTaskDef();
138	    const { service, fakeReceive, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({
139	      definition: def,
140	      sendResults: [makeSentResult()],
141	    });
142	
143	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
144	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
145	
146	    // Emit non-matching event
147	    fakeReceive.emit({
148	      frameId: 'frame-1',
149	      fieldId: 'field-1',
150	      value: 999, // doesn't match threshold 100
151	    });
152	
153	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
154	
155	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(0);
156	
157	    service.stopTask(instance.instanceId);
158	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 100);
159	  });
160	
161	  it('step failure does not reset trigger count', async () => {
162	    const maxTriggerCount = 3;
163	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
164	      ...triggerTaskDef(),
165	      maxTriggerCount,
166	      cooldownMs: 0,
167	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'skip-step' },
168	    };
169	    // First call fails, rest succeed
170	    const results = [makeErrorResult(), ...Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => makeSentResult())];
171	    const { service, fakeReceive, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({
172	      definition: def,
173	      sendResults: results,
174	    });
175	
176	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
177	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
178	
179	    for (let i = 0; i < maxTriggerCount; i++) {
180	      fakeReceive.emit({
181	        frameId: def.triggerCondition!.frameId,
182	        fieldId: def.triggerCondition!.fieldId,
183	        value: def.triggerCondition!.threshold,
184	      });
185	      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
186	    }
187	
188	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
189	
190	    // Should have attempted maxTriggerCount triggers (not more)
191	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(maxTriggerCount * def.steps.length);
192	  });
193	});

199	describe('TaskService - sequence scheduling', () => {
200	  it('executes steps in order then completes', async () => {
201	    const def = sequenceTaskDef();
202	    const results = Array.from({ length: def.steps.length }, () => makeSentResult());
203	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
204	
205	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
206	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId);
207	
208	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
209	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
210	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2); // 2 send steps (delay step doesn't call send)
211	    expect(final!.stepResults.length).toBe(3); // 3 steps total
212	  });
213	
214	  it('respects step ordering', async () => {
215	    const def = sequenceTaskDef();
216	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
217	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
218	
219	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
220	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId);
221	
222	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
223	    // Steps: send(0), delay(1), send(2)
224	    expect(final!.stepResults[0].stepIndex).toBe(0);
225	    expect(final!.stepResults[0].kind).toBe('send');
226	    expect(final!.stepResults[1].stepIndex).toBe(1);
227	    expect(final!.stepResults[1].kind).toBe('delay');
228	    expect(final!.stepResults[2].stepIndex).toBe(2);
229	    expect(final!.stepResults[2].kind).toBe('send');
230	  });
231	});

234	// SCOE pattern
235	// ========================================================================
236	
237	describe('TaskService - SCOE pattern', () => {
238	  it('send → wait-condition → send → completed', async () => {
239	    const def = scoeModeTaskDef();
240	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()]; // 2 send steps
241	    const { service, fakeReceive, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({
242	      definition: def,
243	      sendResults: results,
244	    });
245	
246	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
247	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
248	
249	    // First send step should have executed
250	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(1);
251	
252	    // Emit matching condition for wait-condition-step
253	    const waitStep = def.steps[1];
254	    if (waitStep.kind === 'wait-condition') {
255	      fakeReceive.emit({
256	        frameId: waitStep.waitConfig.condition.frameId,
257	        fieldId: waitStep.waitConfig.condition.fieldId,
258	        value: waitStep.waitConfig.condition.threshold,
259	      });
260	    }
261	
262	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
263	
264	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
265	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
266	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2); // command + ack
267	    expect(final!.stepResults.length).toBe(3);
268	    expect(final!.stepResults[0].kind).toBe('send');
269	    expect(final!.stepResults[1].kind).toBe('wait-condition');
270	    expect(final!.stepResults[1].matched).toBe(true);
271	    expect(final!.stepResults[2].kind).toBe('send');
272	  });
273	});

279	describe('TaskService - wait-condition timeout', () => {
280	  it('onTimeout=continue continues execution', async () => {
281	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
282	      ...scoeModeTaskDef(),
283	      steps: [
284	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
285	        {
286	          id: 's2', kind: 'wait-condition',
287	          waitConfig: { condition: { frameId: 'f1', fieldId: 'f1', operator: 'eq', threshold: 99 }, timeoutMs: 50, onTimeout: 'continue' },
288	        },
289	        { id: 's3', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
290	      ],
291	    };
292	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
293	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
294	
295	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
296	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
297	
298	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
299	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
300	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2);
301	    const waitResult = final!.stepResults.find((r) => r.kind === 'wait-condition');
302	    expect(waitResult).toBeDefined();
303	    if (waitResult?.kind === 'wait-condition') {
304	      expect(waitResult.matched).toBe(false);
305	      expect(waitResult.timedOut).toBe(true);
306	    }
307	  });
308	
309	  it('onTimeout=fail triggers error policy', async () => {
310	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
311	      ...scoeModeTaskDef(),
312	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
313	      steps: [
314	        {
315	          id: 's1', kind: 'wait-condition',
316	          waitConfig: { condition: { frameId: 'f1', fieldId: 'f1', operator: 'eq', threshold: 99 }, timeoutMs: 50, onTimeout: 'fail' },
317	        },
318	      ],
319	    };
320	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def });
321	
322	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
323	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
324	
325	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
326	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('stopped');
327	  });
328	
329	  it('onTimeout=skip skips the step', async () => {
330	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
331	      ...scoeModeTaskDef(),
332	      steps: [
333	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
334	        {
335	          id: 's2', kind: 'wait-condition',
336	          waitConfig: { condition: { frameId: 'f1', fieldId: 'f1', operator: 'eq', threshold: 99 }, timeoutMs: 50, onTimeout: 'skip' },
337	        },
338	        { id: 's3', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
339	      ],
340	    };
341	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
342	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
343	
344	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
345	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
346	
347	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
348	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
349	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2);
350	    const waitResult = final!.stepResults.find((r) => r.kind === 'wait-condition');
351	    expect(waitResult?.appliedPolicy).toBe('skip-step');
352	  });
353	});

359	describe('TaskService - error policies', () => {
360	  it('stop policy stops task on send failure', async () => {
361	    const def: TaskDefinition = { ...timedTaskDef(), errorPolicy: errorPolicies.stopOnFailure(), maxCount: 5, intervalMs: 10 };
362	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: [makeErrorResult()] });
363	
364	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
365	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
366	
367	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
368	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('stopped');
369	  });
370	
371	  it('skip-step policy continues to next step', async () => {
372	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
373	      ...sequenceTaskDef(),
374	      errorPolicy: errorPolicies.skipStep(),
375	    };
376	    // First send fails, second succeeds
377	    const results = [makeErrorResult(), makeSentResult()];
378	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
379	
380	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
381	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
382	
383	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
384	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
385	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2);
386	    const failedResult = final!.stepResults.find((r) => r.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step');
387	    expect(failedResult).toBeDefined();
388	  });
389	
390	  it('pause policy pauses task on failure', async () => {
391	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
392	      ...timedTaskDef(),
393	      errorPolicy: errorPolicies.pauseOnFailure(),
394	      maxCount: 5,
395	      intervalMs: 10,
396	    };
397	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: [makeErrorResult()] });
398	
399	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
400	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
401	
402	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
403	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('paused');
404	  });
405	
406	  it('retry policy retries then fails on exhaustion', async () => {
407	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
408	      id: 'retry-exhaust',
409	      name: 'Retry Exhaust',
410	      schedulingMode: 'sequence',
411	      triggerSource: 'user-ui',
412	      steps: [{ id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } }],
413	      errorPolicy: errorPolicies.retryTwice(),
414	    };
415	    const results = Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => makeErrorResult());
416	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
417	
418	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
419	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 2000);
420	
421	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
422	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('failed');
423	    // 1 original + 2 retries = 3 calls
424	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(3);
425	  });
426	
427	  it('retry policy succeeds on retry', async () => {
428	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
429	      id: 'retry-succeed',
430	      name: 'Retry Succeed',
431	      schedulingMode: 'sequence',
432	      triggerSource: 'user-ui',
433	      steps: [
434	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
435	        { id: 's2', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
436	      ],
437	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'retry', retryCount: 2, retryDelayMs: 10 },
438	    };
439	    // First call fails, second succeeds, third succeeds
440	    const results = [makeErrorResult(), makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
441	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
442	
443	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
444	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 2000);
445	
446	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
447	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
448	  });
449	});

455	describe('TaskService - pause/resume', () => {
456	  it('pause preserves progress', async () => {
457	    // Sequence with 3 steps, pause after first send
458	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
459	      id: 'pause-test',
460	      name: 'Pause Test',
461	      schedulingMode: 'sequence',
462	      triggerSource: 'user-ui',
463	      steps: [
464	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
465	        { id: 's2', kind: 'delay', delayConfig: { durationMs: 500 } },
466	        { id: 's3', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
467	      ],
468	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
469	    };
470	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
471	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
472	
473	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
474	    // Wait for first step to complete
475	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
476	
477	    const running = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
478	    if (running?.lifecycle === 'running') {
479	      service.pauseTask(instance.instanceId);
480	    }
481	
482	    const paused = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
483	    expect(paused?.lifecycle).toBe('paused');
484	    // Should have at least 1 step result
485	    expect(paused!.stepResults.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
486	
487	    service.stopTask(instance.instanceId);
488	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 100);
489	  });
490	
491	  it('resume continues execution', async () => {
492	    const state = createTaskState();
493	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
494	      id: 'resume-test',
495	      name: 'Resume Test',
496	      schedulingMode: 'sequence',
497	      triggerSource: 'user-ui',
498	      steps: [
499	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
500	      ],
501	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
502	    };
503	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
504	    const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results });
505	    const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();
506	    const service = createTaskService({
507	      sendService: fakeSend,
508	      receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,
509	      state,
510	      now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',
511	    });
512	
513	    const inst1 = service.createTask(def);
514	    service.startTask(inst1.instanceId);
515	    await settle(service, inst1.instanceId, 200);
516	    expect(service.getInstance(inst1.instanceId)?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
517	
518	    // Create second task and verify service still works
519	    const inst2 = service.createTask(def);
520	    service.startTask(inst2.instanceId);
521	    await settle(service, inst2.instanceId, 200);
522	    expect(service.getInstance(inst2.instanceId)?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
523	  });
524	});

530	describe('TaskService - stop', () => {
531	  it('stop results in lifecycle=stopped (not completed)', async () => {
532	    const def: TaskDefinition = { ...timedTaskDef(), intervalMs: 100, maxCount: 100 };
533	    const results = Array.from({ length: 200 }, () => makeSentResult());
534	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
535	
536	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);

539	    service.stopTask(instance.instanceId);

541	
542	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);

544	    expect(final?.lifecycle).not.toBe('completed');
