```
548	// ========================================================================
549	// State / Selectors
550	// ========================================================================
551	
552	describe('Task state and selectors', () => {
553	  let state: TaskStateContainer;
554	
555	  beforeEach(() => {
556	    state = createTaskState();
557	  });
558	
559	  it('createInstance adds to instances', () => {
560	    const def = timedTaskDef();
561	    const inst = state.createInstance('inst-1', def);
562	    expect(inst.instanceId).toBe('inst-1');
563	    expect(inst.lifecycle).toBe('created');
564	
565	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
566	    expect(snapshot.instances.length).toBe(1);
567	    expect(snapshot.instances[0].instanceId).toBe('inst-1');
568	  });
569	
570	  it('updateInstance modifies lifecycle', () => {
571	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
572	    const updated = state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'running', startedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
573	    expect(updated?.lifecycle).toBe('running');
574	    expect(updated?.startedAt).toBe('2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z');
575	  });
576	
577	  it('addStepResult appends and bounds history', () => {
578	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
579	    for (let i = 0; i < 150; i++) {
580	      state.addStepResult('inst-1', {
581	        kind: 'send',
582	        stepIndex: 0,
583	        iteration: i,
584	        sendResult: makeSentResult(),
585	      });
586	    }
587	    const inst = state.getInstance('inst-1')!;
588	    // Should keep only results from iterations >= 50 (150 - 100 + 1)
589	    const minIter = Math.max(0, 149 - 100 + 1);
590	    expect(inst.stepResults.length).toBe(149 - minIter + 1);
591	    expect(inst.stepResults.every((r) => r.iteration >= minIter)).toBe(true);
592	  });
593	
594	  it('moveToHistory moves terminal instances', () => {
595	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
596	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'completed', completedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
597	    state.moveToHistory('inst-1');
598	
599	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
600	    expect(snapshot.instances.length).toBe(0);
601	    expect(snapshot.history.length).toBe(1);
602	    expect(snapshot.history[0].instanceId).toBe('inst-1');
603	  });
604	
605	  it('removeInstance removes from state', () => {
606	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
607	    state.removeInstance('inst-1');
608	    expect(state.getInstance('inst-1')).toBeUndefined();
609	  });
610	
611	  it('getSnapshot returns independent clones', () => {
612	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
613	    const s1 = state.getSnapshot();
614	    const s2 = state.getSnapshot();
615	    expect(s1).toEqual(s2);
616	    expect(s1.instances).not.toBe(s2.instances);
617	  });
618	
619	  it('statistics update correctly', () => {
620	    const def = timedTaskDef();
621	    state.createInstance('inst-1', def);
622	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalCreated).toBe(1);
623	
624	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'running', startedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
625	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'completed', completedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:01.000Z' });
626	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalCompleted).toBe(1);
627	
628	    state.createInstance('inst-2', def);
629	    state.updateInstance('inst-2', { lifecycle: 'running', startedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
630	    state.updateInstance('inst-2', { lifecycle: 'stopped', stoppedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:01.000Z' });
631	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalStopped).toBe(1);
632	  });
633	
634	  it('step statistics track succeeded/failed/skipped', () => {
635	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
636	    state.addStepResult('inst-1', { kind: 'send', stepIndex: 0, iteration: 0, sendResult: makeSentResult() });
637	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalStepsSucceeded).toBe(1);
638	
639	    state.addStepResult('inst-1', { kind: 'send', stepIndex: 1, iteration: 0, sendResult: makeErrorResult(), appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' });
640	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalStepsSkipped).toBe(1);
641	
642	    state.addStepResult('inst-1', { kind: 'send', stepIndex: 2, iteration: 0, sendResult: makeErrorResult() });
643	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalStepsFailed).toBe(1);
644	  });
645	
646	  it('resetStats clears statistics', () => {
647	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
648	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'completed', completedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
649	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalCreated).toBe(1);
650	
651	    state.resetStats();
652	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalCreated).toBe(0);
653	  });
654	});
```

```
1	import type { WaitCondition, ComparisonOperator, ConditionMatchInput } from './types';
2	
3	export function evaluateCondition(
4	  condition: WaitCondition,
5	  input: ConditionMatchInput,
6	): boolean {
7	  if (input.frameId !== condition.frameId || input.fieldId !== condition.fieldId) {
8	    return false;
9	  }
10	  if (condition.sourceId !== undefined && input.sourceId !== undefined && condition.sourceId !== input.sourceId) {
11	    return false;
12	  }
13	  return applyOperator(condition.operator, input.value, condition.threshold);
14	}
15	
16	function applyOperator(
17	  operator: ComparisonOperator,
18	  value: number | string | null,
19	  threshold: number | string,
20	): boolean {
21	  switch (operator) {
22	    case 'any':
23	      return true;
24	    case 'change':
25	      return value !== null;
26	    case 'eq':
27	      return value !== null && compareValues(value, threshold) === 0;
28	    case 'neq':
29	      return value !== null && compareValues(value, threshold) !== 0;
30	    case 'gt':
31	      return value !== null && compareValues(value, threshold) > 0;
32	    case 'lt':
33	      return value !== null && compareValues(value, threshold) < 0;
34	    case 'gte':
35	      return value !== null && compareValues(value, threshold) >= 0;
36	    case 'lte':
37	      return value !== null && compareValues(value, threshold) <= 0;
38	  }
39	}
40	
41	export function compareValues(
42	  a: number | string | null,
43	  b: number | string,
44	): number {
45	  if (a === null) return -1;
46	  if (typeof a === 'number' && typeof b === 'number') {
47	    return a - b;
48	  }
49	  const sa = typeof a === 'number' ? String(a) : a;
50	  const sb = typeof b === 'number' ? String(b) : b;
51	  return sa < sb ? -1 : sa > sb ? 1 : 0;
52	}
53
```

```
1	import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';
2	import type { ReceiveEventSource } from './ports';
3	
4	export interface FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions {
5	  readonly autoEmit?: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
6	  readonly autoEmitIntervalMs?: number;
7	}
8	
9	export function createFakeReceiveEventSource(
10	  _options?: FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions,
11	): ReceiveEventSource & {
12	  emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void;
13	  readonly emitted: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
14	} {
15	  const emitted: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
16	  const handlers = new Set<(input: ConditionMatchInput) => void>();
17	
18	  return {
19	    get emitted() {
20	      return emitted;
21	    },
22	
23	    emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
24	      emitted.push(input);
25	      for (const handler of handlers) {
26	        handler(input);
27	      }
28	    },
29	
30	    subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void {
31	      handlers.add(handler);
32	      return () => {
33	        handlers.delete(handler);
34	      };
35	    },
36	  };
37	}
38
```

```
1	import type { SendRequest, SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type { SendServiceProvider } from './ports';
3	
4	export interface FakeSendServiceOptions {
5	  readonly results?: readonly SendResult[];
6	  readonly delayMs?: number;
7	}
8	
9	export interface RecordedCall {
10	  readonly request: SendRequest;
11	  readonly index: number;
12	}
13	
14	function defaultSentResult(): SendResult {
15	  return {
16	    kind: 'sent',
17	    requestRef: { frameId: 'frame-1', targetId: 'target-1', context: { source: 'task' } },
18	    bytesBuilt: 10,
19	    bytesSent: 10,
20	    timestamp: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',
21	    buildIssues: [],
22	  };
23	}
24	
25	export function createFakeSendService(
26	  options: FakeSendServiceOptions = {},
27	): SendServiceProvider & { readonly calls: readonly RecordedCall[] } {
28	  const calls: RecordedCall[] = [];
29	  const results = options.results ?? [defaultSentResult()];
30	  const delayMs = options.delayMs ?? 0;
31	  let callIndex = 0;
32	
33	  return {
34	    get calls() {
35	      return calls;
36	    },
37	
38	    async execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult> {
39	      const index = callIndex;
40	      calls.push({ request, index });
41	      callIndex++;
42	      if (delayMs > 0) {
43	        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs));
44	      }
45	      const result = index < results.length ? results[index] : results[results.length - 1];
46	      return { ...result };
47	    },
48	  };
49	}
50	
51	export function createFailingSendService(
52	  errorKind: string,
53	  errorMessage: string,
54	): SendServiceProvider {
55	  return {
56	    async execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult> {
57	      return {
58	        kind: 'transport-error',
59	        requestRef: {
60	          frameId: request.frameId,
61	          targetId: request.targetId,
62	          context: { ...request.context },
63	        },
64	        bytesBuilt: 0,
65	        bytesSent: 0,
66	        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
67	        error: { kind: errorKind, message: errorMessage },
68	        buildIssues: [],
69	      };
70	    },
71	  };
72	}
73
```

```
1	import type { TaskLifecycleStatus, LifecycleAction } from './types';
2	
3	const VALID_TRANSITIONS: Record<TaskLifecycleStatus, readonly TaskLifecycleStatus[]> = {
4	  created: ['running'],
5	  running: ['paused', 'stopped', 'completed', 'failed'],
6	  paused: ['running', 'stopped'],
7	  stopped: [],
8	  completed: [],
9	  failed: [],
10	};
11	
12	const ACTION_TARGETS: Record<LifecycleAction, readonly { from: TaskLifecycleStatus; to: TaskLifecycleStatus }[]> = {
13	  start: [{ from: 'created', to: 'running' }],
14	  pause: [{ from: 'running', to: 'paused' }],
15	  resume: [{ from: 'paused', to: 'running' }],
16	  stop: [{ from: 'running', to: 'stopped' }, { from: 'paused', to: 'stopped' }],
17	  complete: [{ from: 'running', to: 'completed' }],
18	  fail: [{ from: 'running', to: 'failed' }],
19	};
20	
21	export function canTransition(from: TaskLifecycleStatus, to: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean {
22	  return VALID_TRANSITIONS[from].includes(to);
23	}
24	
25	export function transition(state: TaskLifecycleStatus, action: LifecycleAction): TaskLifecycleStatus {
26	  const targets = ACTION_TARGETS[action];
27	  const match = targets.find((t) => t.from === state);
28	  if (!match) {
29	    throw new Error(`Invalid lifecycle transition: cannot '${action}' from '${state}'`);
30	  }
31	  return match.to;
32	}
33	
34	export function isTerminal(status: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean {
35	  return status === 'stopped' || status === 'completed' || status === 'failed';
36	}
37	
```

> AGENT

Found the trigger bug. In `runTriggerLoop`, the handler is registered via `conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler)`, but `processInput` calls `entry.onMatch(input.value)` — passing the raw value, not the full input. The handler then tries to call `evaluateCondition(triggerCondition, input)` on that raw value, which fails because a number has no `.frameId`.

The condition was already evaluated by `processInput`, so the re-evaluation is redundant and broken.
